# WebflowサイトにllMs.txtを追加する方法（ステップバイステップガイド）

公開:20 1, 2026 \|  at 11:00 午前

# WebflowサイトにLlms.txtを追加する方法（ステップバイステップガイド）

Webflowはデザイナーの夢です - クリーンなコードを犠牲にすることなく、強力なビジュアル開発が可能です。Webflowで構築している場合、llms.txtファイルの追加にはカスタムアプローチが必要ですが、完全に実行可能で、AI最適化を完全にコントロールできます。

この包括的なガイドでは、推奨される静的ページアプローチから高度なカスタムコードソリューションまで、Webflowでllms.txtを実装する2つの実証済みの方法を説明します。

## 目次 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E7%9B%AE%E6%AC%A1)

## WebflowサイトにLlms.txtが必要な理由 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#webflow%E3%82%B5%E3%82%A4%E3%83%88%E3%81%ABllmstxt%E3%81%8C%E5%BF%85%E8%A6%81%E3%81%AA%E7%90%86%E7%94%B1)

### デザイナーの利点 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E3%83%87%E3%82%B6%E3%82%A4%E3%83%8A%E3%83%BC%E3%81%AE%E5%88%A9%E7%82%B9)

Webflowは、サイトのデザインを完璧にピクセル単位でコントロールできます。llms.txtファイルは、そのコントロールをAIシステムがあなたの作品を理解し推薦する方法にまで拡張します：

- **ポートフォリオの可視性**: AIアシスタントがあなたのデザイン作品を推薦するのを支援
- **クライアント獲得**: 潜在的なクライアントがデザイナーをAIに尋ねた時に表示される
- **サービス発見**: 提供内容の明確な構造
- **プロジェクト紹介**: 最高の作品への整理されたアクセス

### Webflow固有の利点 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#webflow%E5%9B%BA%E6%9C%89%E3%81%AE%E5%88%A9%E7%82%B9)

- ✅ **クリーンな実装**: 肥大化したプラグイン不要
- ✅ **完全なコントロール**: 正確に希望通りにカスタマイズ
- ✅ **高速読み込み**: 最小限のオーバーヘッド
- ✅ **プロフェッショナル**: 適切なセマンティック構造

## 方法1: 静的ページアプローチ（推奨） [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E6%96%B9%E6%B3%951-%E9%9D%99%E7%9A%84%E3%83%9A%E3%83%BC%E3%82%B8%E3%82%A2%E3%83%97%E3%83%AD%E3%83%BC%E3%83%81%E6%8E%A8%E5%A5%A8)

**最適な対象**: ほとんどのWebflowユーザー
**所要時間**: 10-15分
**技術レベル**: ⭐⭐ 中級

この方法は、llms.txtコンテンツを提供する専用ページを作成します。

### ステップ1: 新しい静的ページを作成 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E3%82%B9%E3%83%86%E3%83%83%E3%83%971-%E6%96%B0%E3%81%97%E3%81%84%E9%9D%99%E7%9A%84%E3%83%9A%E3%83%BC%E3%82%B8%E3%82%92%E4%BD%9C%E6%88%90)

1. Webflowプロジェクトを開く
2. **ページ** パネル（左サイドバー）で、 **+アイコン** をクリック
3. **ページを追加** をクリック
4. **ページ名** を設定: `llms`
5. システムがURL `/llms`を作成

### ステップ2: ページ設定を構成 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E3%82%B9%E3%83%86%E3%83%83%E3%83%972-%E3%83%9A%E3%83%BC%E3%82%B8%E8%A8%AD%E5%AE%9A%E3%82%92%E6%A7%8B%E6%88%90)

1. 新しいページを選択し、 **設定**（歯車アイコン）をクリック
2. **SEO設定** セクションで:

   - **ページタイトル**: 空白のまま
   - **メタ説明**: 空白のまま
   - **スラッグ**: `llms`から`llms.txt`に変更
3. URLは`/llms.txt`になります

### ステップ3: ページをデザイン [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E3%82%B9%E3%83%86%E3%83%83%E3%83%973-%E3%83%9A%E3%83%BC%E3%82%B8%E3%82%92%E3%83%87%E3%82%B6%E3%82%A4%E3%83%B3)

1. **デフォルト要素を削除**
   - デフォルトのセクションやコンテナを削除
   - 完全に空白のページで開始
2. **コード埋め込み要素を追加**
   - `E`を押すか、 **要素を追加**(+)をクリック
   - **コンポーネント** までスクロール
   - **埋め込み** をページにドラッグ
   - 埋め込み設定で **コードを編集** をクリック
3. **llms.txtコンテンツを追加**

   このコードを貼り付けます:


```
<pre
  style="white-space: pre-wrap; font-family: monospace; margin: 0; padding: 0; width: 100%; background: white; color: black;"
>
# あなたのデザインスタジオ名

> [あなたのニッチ]向けの美しく機能的なウェブサイトを作成

## サービス

- [ウェブデザイン](/services/web-design) - カスタムウェブサイトデザイン
- [ブランディング](/services/branding) - ブランドアイデンティティとデザインシステム
- [開発](/services/development) - Webflow開発

## ポートフォリオ

- [注目プロジェクト](/portfolio) - 最高の作品
- [クライアントギャラリー](/clients) - クライアントの声と結果
- [ケーススタディ](/case-studies) - 詳細なプロジェクト分析

## について

- [プロセス](/process) - 作業方法
- [私たちについて](/about) - ストーリーとチーム
- [お問い合わせ](/contact) - プロジェクトを開始

## リソース

- [ブログ](/blog) - デザインの洞察とチュートリアル
- [無料リソース](/resources) - テンプレートとガイド
- [FAQ](/faq) - よくある質問
</pre>コピー
```

**カスタマイズ**:

- プレースホルダーテキストを実際のコンテンツに置き換え
- URLをサイト構造に合わせて更新
- 必要に応じてセクションを追加/削除

### ステップ4: Content-Typeヘッダーを設定 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E3%82%B9%E3%83%86%E3%83%83%E3%83%974-content-type%E3%83%98%E3%83%83%E3%83%80%E3%83%BC%E3%82%92%E8%A8%AD%E5%AE%9A)

適切なテキスト表示を確保するため、ページのカスタムコードに以下を追加:

1. llmsページを選択し、 **ページ設定** を開く
2. **カスタムコード** タブへ移動
3. **タグの前** に追加:

```
<script>
  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    // コンテンツタイプのメタタグを設定
    var meta = document.createElement("meta");
    meta.httpEquiv = "Content-Type";
    meta.content = "text/plain; charset=utf-8";
    document.head.appendChild(meta);
  });
</script>コピー
```

### ステップ5: ナビゲーションから非表示 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E3%82%B9%E3%83%86%E3%83%83%E3%83%975-%E3%83%8A%E3%83%93%E3%82%B2%E3%83%BC%E3%82%B7%E3%83%A7%E3%83%B3%E3%81%8B%E3%82%89%E9%9D%9E%E8%A1%A8%E7%A4%BA)

1. **ページ** パネルへ移動
2. `llms.txt`ページを右クリック
3. **ナビゲーションに表示** のチェックを外す

これにより、アクセス可能でありながらメニューに表示されません。

### ステップ6: 公開とテスト [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E3%82%B9%E3%83%86%E3%83%83%E3%83%976-%E5%85%AC%E9%96%8B%E3%81%A8%E3%83%86%E3%82%B9%E3%83%88)

1. **公開**（右上）をクリック
2. **\[あなたのドメイン\]に公開** を選択
3. 新しいタブを開き、`yoursite.com/llms.txt`にアクセス
4. コンテンツがプレーンテキストとして表示されることを確認

## 方法2: カスタムコード実装（上級者向け） [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E6%96%B9%E6%B3%952-%E3%82%AB%E3%82%B9%E3%82%BF%E3%83%A0%E3%82%B3%E3%83%BC%E3%83%89%E5%AE%9F%E8%A3%85%E4%B8%8A%E7%B4%9A%E8%80%85%E5%90%91%E3%81%91)

**最適な対象**: Webflowホスティングアクセス権を持つ開発者
**所要時間**: 20-30分
**技術レベル**: ⭐⭐⭐ 上級

この方法では、サイトをエクスポートして真の`.txt`ファイルを追加します。

### ステップ1: Webflowサイトをエクスポート [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E3%82%B9%E3%83%86%E3%83%83%E3%83%971-webflow%E3%82%B5%E3%82%A4%E3%83%88%E3%82%92%E3%82%A8%E3%82%AF%E3%82%B9%E3%83%9D%E3%83%BC%E3%83%88)

1. **プロジェクト設定** \> **エクスポート** へ移動
2. **ZIPを準備** をクリック
3. サイトファイルをダウンロード
4. ZIPファイルを展開

### ステップ2: Llms.txtファイルを作成 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E3%82%B9%E3%83%86%E3%83%83%E3%83%972-llmstxt%E3%83%95%E3%82%A1%E3%82%A4%E3%83%AB%E3%82%92%E4%BD%9C%E6%88%90)

1. テキストエディタ（VS Code、Sublimeなど）を開く
2. `llms.txt`という新しいファイルを作成
3. Markdown形式でコンテンツを追加:

```
# あなたのWebflowサイト名

> 何をするかの一文説明

## サービス

- [サービス1](/service-1) - 説明
- [サービス2](/service-2) - 説明

## 作品

- [ポートフォリオ](/portfolio) - プロジェクトを見る
- [ケーススタディ](/case-studies) - 詳細な分析

## お問い合わせ

- [連絡する](/contact) - プロジェクトを開始
- [について](/about) - 私たちについて学ぶコピー
```

### ステップ3: ルートディレクトリに追加 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E3%82%B9%E3%83%86%E3%83%83%E3%83%973-%E3%83%AB%E3%83%BC%E3%83%88%E3%83%87%E3%82%A3%E3%83%AC%E3%82%AF%E3%83%88%E3%83%AA%E3%81%AB%E8%BF%BD%E5%8A%A0)

1. 展開したサイトフォルダのルートに`llms.txt`を配置
2. ファイルは`index.html`と同じレベルにあるべき

### ステップ4: ホスティングに再アップロード [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E3%82%B9%E3%83%86%E3%83%83%E3%83%974-%E3%83%9B%E3%82%B9%E3%83%86%E3%82%A3%E3%83%B3%E3%82%B0%E3%81%AB%E5%86%8D%E3%82%A2%E3%83%83%E3%83%97%E3%83%AD%E3%83%BC%E3%83%89)

**Webflowホスティングの場合**:
残念ながら、Webflowホスティングは直接ファイルアップロードを許可していません。次のいずれかが必要:

1. 代わりに方法1（静的ページ）を使用、または
2. カスタムホスティングを使用（以下参照）

**カスタムホスティング**（AWS、Netlify、Vercelなど）の場合:

1. 変更したサイトファイルをアップロード
2. `llms.txt`を`text/plain`として提供するようホストを構成
3. デプロイ

### ステップ5: サーバーヘッダーを構成 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E3%82%B9%E3%83%86%E3%83%83%E3%83%975-%E3%82%B5%E3%83%BC%E3%83%90%E3%83%BC%E3%83%98%E3%83%83%E3%83%80%E3%83%BC%E3%82%92%E6%A7%8B%E6%88%90)

適切なコンテンツタイプのためにこれらのヘッダーを追加:

**Netlify** (`_headers`ファイル):

```
/llms.txt
  Content-Type: text/plain; charset=utf-8コピー
```

**Vercel** (`vercel.json`):

```
{
  "headers": [\
    {\
      "source": "/llms.txt",\
      "headers": [\
        {\
          "key": "Content-Type",\
          "value": "text/plain; charset=utf-8"\
        }\
      ]\
    }\
  ]
}コピー
```

**AWS S3**:
アップロード時にメタデータを設定: `Content-Type: text/plain`

## Webflow固有のテンプレート [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#webflow%E5%9B%BA%E6%9C%89%E3%81%AE%E3%83%86%E3%83%B3%E3%83%97%E3%83%AC%E3%83%BC%E3%83%88)

### デザインエージェンシー向け [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E3%83%87%E3%82%B6%E3%82%A4%E3%83%B3%E3%82%A8%E3%83%BC%E3%82%B8%E3%82%A7%E3%83%B3%E3%82%B7%E3%83%BC%E5%90%91%E3%81%91)

```
# [エージェンシー名] - デジタルデザインスタジオ

> 野心的なブランドのための受賞歴のあるウェブデザインと開発

## サービス

- [ウェブデザイン](/services/web-design) - カスタム、レスポンシブウェブサイト
- [UX/UIデザイン](/services/ux-ui) - ユーザー中心のデザイン
- [Webflow開発](/services/webflow) - ノーコード開発
- [ブランディング](/services/branding) - ビジュアルアイデンティティシステム
```

## Webflow Llms.txtの最適化 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#webflow-llmstxt%E3%81%AE%E6%9C%80%E9%81%A9%E5%8C%96)

### 1\. 構造をクリーンに保つ [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#1-%E6%A7%8B%E9%80%A0%E3%82%92%E3%82%AF%E3%83%AA%E3%83%BC%E3%83%B3%E3%81%AB%E4%BF%9D%E3%81%A4)

Webflowのクリーンなコード哲学はllms.txtにも拡張:

```
# 明確な階層

## 第2レベルセクション

### 必要に応じて第3レベル

- 説明付きリスト項目コピー
```

### 2\. デザインシステムに合わせる [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#2-%E3%83%87%E3%82%B6%E3%82%A4%E3%83%B3%E3%82%B7%E3%82%B9%E3%83%86%E3%83%A0%E3%81%AB%E5%90%88%E3%82%8F%E3%81%9B%E3%82%8B)

サイトのナビゲーションを反映するようにllms.txtを整理:

- 主要なサービス/製品を最初に
- ポートフォリオ/作品サンプル
- について/会社情報
- リソースとサポート

### 3\. 説明的なリンクを使用 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#3-%E8%AA%AC%E6%98%8E%E7%9A%84%E3%81%AA%E3%83%AA%E3%83%B3%E3%82%AF%E3%82%92%E4%BD%BF%E7%94%A8)

```
❌ - [こちらをクリック](/services)
✅ - [ウェブデザインサービス](/services/web-design) - スタートアップ向けカスタムウェブサイトコピー
```

### 4\. Webflow CMSを活用 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#4-webflow-cms%E3%82%92%E6%B4%BB%E7%94%A8)

Webflow CMSを使用する場合:

1. 「重要なページ」用のCMSコレクションを作成
2. llms.txtに含めたい項目を追加
3. カスタムコードを使用してプルと表示
4. CMS変更で自動更新

### 5\. ブレークポイント全体でテスト [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#5-%E3%83%96%E3%83%AC%E3%83%BC%E3%82%AF%E3%83%9D%E3%82%A4%E3%83%B3%E3%83%88%E5%85%A8%E4%BD%93%E3%81%A7%E3%83%86%E3%82%B9%E3%83%88)

Webflowのレスポンシブデザインを使用しているため:

1. デスクトップ、タブレット、モバイルでllms.txtをテスト
2. `<pre>`タグが正しく表示されることを確認
3. 空白処理を確認
4. 水平スクロールがないことを確認

## Webflow固有のベストプラクティス [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#webflow%E5%9B%BA%E6%9C%89%E3%81%AE%E3%83%99%E3%82%B9%E3%83%88%E3%83%97%E3%83%A9%E3%82%AF%E3%83%86%E3%82%A3%E3%82%B9)

### 1\. WebflowのSEO設定を使用 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#1-webflow%E3%81%AEseo%E8%A8%AD%E5%AE%9A%E3%82%92%E4%BD%BF%E7%94%A8)

llms.txtに表示されるページを最適化:

1. 各ページの **ページ設定** へ移動
2. 明確な **ページタイトル** を追加
3. 簡潔な **メタ説明** を書く
4. 適切な **OpenGraph** タグを使用

### 2\. コレクションを整理 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#2-%E3%82%B3%E3%83%AC%E3%82%AF%E3%82%B7%E3%83%A7%E3%83%B3%E3%82%92%E6%95%B4%E7%90%86)

Webflow CMSコレクションを使用する場合:

1. 論理的なコレクション構造を作成
2. 明確なコレクション名を使用
3. コレクション項目に説明を追加
4. llms.txtのメインコレクションページにリンク

### 3\. インタラクションを活用（慎重に） [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#3-%E3%82%A4%E3%83%B3%E3%82%BF%E3%83%A9%E3%82%AF%E3%82%B7%E3%83%A7%E3%83%B3%E3%82%92%E6%B4%BB%E7%94%A8%E6%85%8E%E9%87%8D%E3%81%AB)

llms.txtページをシンプルに保つ:

- ❌ アニメーションやインタラクションなし
- ❌ 複雑なレイアウトなし
- ✅ シンプルで読みやすいテキスト
- ✅ 高速読み込み

### 4\. バージョン管理 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#4-%E3%83%90%E3%83%BC%E3%82%B8%E3%83%A7%E3%83%B3%E7%AE%A1%E7%90%86)

Webflowには組み込みのバージョン管理がないため:

1. llms.txtコンテンツのコピーを外部に保管
2. スプレッドシートで変更を文書化
3. 更新内容とタイミングを記録
4. パフォーマンスの変化を追跡

### 5\. クライアントサイト [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#5-%E3%82%AF%E3%83%A9%E3%82%A4%E3%82%A2%E3%83%B3%E3%83%88%E3%82%B5%E3%82%A4%E3%83%88)

クライアント向けに構築する場合:

1. 提案でllms.txtの利点を説明
2. 成果物にセットアップを含める
3. クライアントにコンテンツ更新方法を示す
4. サイトメンテナンスパッケージに追加

## よくあるWebflowの問題と解決策 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E3%82%88%E3%81%8F%E3%81%82%E3%82%8Bwebflow%E3%81%AE%E5%95%8F%E9%A1%8C%E3%81%A8%E8%A7%A3%E6%B1%BA%E7%AD%96)

### 問題: ページにWebflowブランディングが表示される [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E5%95%8F%E9%A1%8C-%E3%83%9A%E3%83%BC%E3%82%B8%E3%81%ABwebflow%E3%83%96%E3%83%A9%E3%83%B3%E3%83%87%E3%82%A3%E3%83%B3%E3%82%B0%E3%81%8C%E8%A1%A8%E7%A4%BA%E3%81%95%E3%82%8C%E3%82%8B)

**問題**: デフォルトのWebflowフッターが表示される

**解決策**:

1. ページからすべてのセクションを削除
2. 埋め込み要素のみを使用
3. ページパディングを0に設定
4. このページのサイトナビゲーション/フッターを非表示

### 問題: コンテンツがプレーンテキストではない [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E5%95%8F%E9%A1%8C-%E3%82%B3%E3%83%B3%E3%83%86%E3%83%B3%E3%83%84%E3%81%8C%E3%83%97%E3%83%AC%E3%83%BC%E3%83%B3%E3%83%86%E3%82%AD%E3%82%B9%E3%83%88%E3%81%A7%E3%81%AF%E3%81%AA%E3%81%84)

**問題**: スタイル付きテキストまたはWebflowフォーマットが表示される

**解決策**:

1. インラインスタイルで`<pre>`タグを使用
2. `background: white`と`color: black`を設定
3. すべてのWebflowクラスを削除
4. テキストブロックではなく、埋め込みコンポーネントを使用

### 問題: URL拡張子を変更できない [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E5%95%8F%E9%A1%8C-url%E6%8B%A1%E5%BC%B5%E5%AD%90%E3%82%92%E5%A4%89%E6%9B%B4%E3%81%A7%E3%81%8D%E3%81%AA%E3%81%84)

**問題**: Webflowが末尾のスラッシュを追加

**解決策**:

1. ページスラッグを正確に`llms.txt`に設定
2. Webflowは`/llms.txt`で提供
3. 末尾のスラッシュありとなしでテスト
4. 両方とも機能するはず

### 問題: カスタムドメインで404 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E5%95%8F%E9%A1%8C-%E3%82%AB%E3%82%B9%E3%82%BF%E3%83%A0%E3%83%89%E3%83%A1%E3%82%A4%E3%83%B3%E3%81%A7404)

**問題**: webflow.ioでは動作するがカスタムドメインでは動作しない

**解決策**:

1. サイトを完全に再公開
2. Webflowキャッシュをクリア
3. DNS設定を確認
4. 伝播のために10-15分待つ

### 問題: モバイル表示の問題 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E5%95%8F%E9%A1%8C-%E3%83%A2%E3%83%90%E3%82%A4%E3%83%AB%E8%A1%A8%E7%A4%BA%E3%81%AE%E5%95%8F%E9%A1%8C)

**問題**: テキストが小さすぎるか、モバイルで奇妙にフォーマットされている

**解決策**:

```
<pre
  style="
  white-space: pre-wrap;
  font-family: monospace;
  font-size: 14px;
  line-height: 1.6;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 20px;
  width: 100%;
  overflow-x: auto;
"
>
ここにコンテンツ
</pre>コピー
```

## パフォーマンス最適化 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E3%83%91%E3%83%95%E3%82%A9%E3%83%BC%E3%83%9E%E3%83%B3%E3%82%B9%E6%9C%80%E9%81%A9%E5%8C%96)

### 軽量に保つ [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E8%BB%BD%E9%87%8F%E3%81%AB%E4%BF%9D%E3%81%A4)

llms.txtは即座に読み込むべき:

- 目標ファイルサイズ: < 10KB
- 画像や外部リソースなし
- 最小限のHTML構造
- 直接的なコンテンツ配信

### キャッシング戦略 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E3%82%AD%E3%83%A3%E3%83%83%E3%82%B7%E3%83%B3%E3%82%B0%E6%88%A6%E7%95%A5)

キャッシングヘッダーを構成（カスタムホスティングの場合）:

```
Cache-Control: public, max-age=3600コピー
```

これは1時間キャッシュ - 新鮮さとパフォーマンスのバランス。

### CDNの考慮事項 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#cdn%E3%81%AE%E8%80%83%E6%85%AE%E4%BA%8B%E9%A0%85)

Cloudflareまたはその他のCDNを使用する場合:

1. llms.txtがCDNを通過することを確認
2. 適切なキャッシュルールを設定
3. `text/plain`コンテンツタイプとして構成
4. 更新後にキャッシュをパージ

## Webflow実装のテスト [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#webflow%E5%AE%9F%E8%A3%85%E3%81%AE%E3%83%86%E3%82%B9%E3%83%88)

### 包括的なテストチェックリスト [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E5%8C%85%E6%8B%AC%E7%9A%84%E3%81%AA%E3%83%86%E3%82%B9%E3%83%88%E3%83%81%E3%82%A7%E3%83%83%E3%82%AF%E3%83%AA%E3%82%B9%E3%83%88)

1. **直接アクセス**
   - ✅ `yoursite.com/llms.txt`が読み込まれる
   - ✅ コンテンツがプレーンテキストとして表示
   - ✅ Webflow UI要素が表示されない
2. **コンテンツ検証**
   - ✅ すべてのリンクが絶対または相対
   - ✅ リンクが正しいページに移動
   - ✅ 壊れたリンクなし
   - ✅ Markdownフォーマットが正しい
3. **クロスブラウザテスト**
   - ✅ Chrome
   - ✅ Firefox
   - ✅ Safari
   - ✅ Edge
4. **デバイステスト**
   - ✅ デスクトップ
   - ✅ タブレット
   - ✅ モバイル
5. **パフォーマンス**
   - ✅ 1秒未満で読み込み
   - ✅ コンソールエラーなし
   - ✅ 適切なcontent-typeヘッダー

## メンテナンスと更新 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E3%83%A1%E3%83%B3%E3%83%86%E3%83%8A%E3%83%B3%E3%82%B9%E3%81%A8%E6%9B%B4%E6%96%B0)

### 定期的な更新スケジュール [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E5%AE%9A%E6%9C%9F%E7%9A%84%E3%81%AA%E6%9B%B4%E6%96%B0%E3%82%B9%E3%82%B1%E3%82%B8%E3%83%A5%E3%83%BC%E3%83%AB)

**毎月**:

- 新しいページ/プロジェクトをレビュー
- 説明を更新
- 壊れたリンクを確認

**四半期ごと**:

- 完全なコンテンツ監査
- 必要に応じて再構成
- すべての機能をテスト

**主要更新後**:

- 新製品/サービスのローンチ
- サイトリデザイン
- 新しいポートフォリオ作品

### 更新プロセス [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E6%9B%B4%E6%96%B0%E3%83%97%E3%83%AD%E3%82%BB%E3%82%B9)

1. **Webflowデザイナーで編集**
   - llmsページを開く
   - 埋め込みコードを編集
   - 変更を保存
2. **プレビューでテスト**
   - Webflowプレビューモードを使用
   - フォーマットを確認
   - リンクを検証
3. **公開**
   - サイトに公開
   - キャッシュをクリア
   - ライブバージョンをテスト

### バージョン追跡 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E3%83%90%E3%83%BC%E3%82%B8%E3%83%A7%E3%83%B3%E8%BF%BD%E8%B7%A1)

変更ログを保持:

```
## llms.txt変更ログ

### 2025-10-05

- 新サービスを追加: Eコマース開発
- ポートフォリオセクションを更新
- aboutページへの壊れたリンクを修正

### 2025-09-15

- 初期実装
- すべての主要サービスを追加
- ポートフォリオセクションを作成コピー
```

## 結論: Webflow + Llms.txt = デザイナーの選択 [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E7%B5%90%E8%AB%96-webflow--llmstxt--%E3%83%87%E3%82%B6%E3%82%A4%E3%83%8A%E3%83%BC%E3%81%AE%E9%81%B8%E6%8A%9E)

Webflowの柔軟性により、llms.txt実装は簡単でありながら、完全なデザインコントロールを提供します。静的ページ方法を選択するか、カスタムホスティングアプローチを選択するかにかかわらず、AI発見のためにサイトを配置しています。

### クイックアクションプラン [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E3%82%AF%E3%82%A4%E3%83%83%E3%82%AF%E3%82%A2%E3%82%AF%E3%82%B7%E3%83%A7%E3%83%B3%E3%83%97%E3%83%A9%E3%83%B3)

**今日（15分）**:

1. ✅ Webflowでllms.txtページを作成
2. ✅ 埋め込み方法を使用してコンテンツを追加
3. ✅ ページ設定を構成
4. ✅ 公開とテスト

**今週（30分）**:

1. ✅ ページ説明を最適化
2. ✅ すべてのリンクをテスト
3. ✅ モバイル表示をレビュー
4. ✅ robots.txtに追加

**継続的**:

1. ✅ 新しいコンテンツで毎月更新
2. ✅ 分析を監視
3. ✅ 結果に基づいて改善

### 重要なポイント [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/ja/blog/webflow-llms-txt-implementation-guide/\#%E9%87%8D%E8%A6%81%E3%81%AA%E3%83%9D%E3%82%A4%E3%83%B3%E3%83%88)

- ✅ Webflowはプラグインなしでllms.txtを実現可能に
- ✅ 静的ページ方法はほとんどのユーザーに有効
- ✅ 構造をクリーンでシンプルに保つ
- ✅ デバイス全体で徹底的にテスト
- ✅ 新しいコンテンツで定期的に更新

あなたのWebflowサイトはAIアシスタントに発見される価値があります。これらの方法で、今日llms.txtを実装し、次世代の検索者にあなたの作品を紹介できます。

* * *

_他のプラットフォームで構築していますか？ [Wix](https://llmgenerator.com/blog/how-to-add-llms-txt-wix)、 [WordPress](https://llmgenerator.com/blog/how-to-add-llms-txt-wordpress)、 [Shopify](https://llmgenerator.com/blog/how-to-add-llms-txt-shopify) のガイドをご覧ください。_

_自動生成が必要ですか？ [LLMGenerator](https://llmgenerator.com/) を試して、Webflowサイト用の最適化されたllms.txtファイルを数分で作成してください。_

## よくある質問

LLMGeneratorとllms.txtについて知っておくべきこと

### llms.txtとは何ですか？なぜ必要なのですか？

llms.txtは、ChatGPT、Claude、その他の大規模言語モデル（LLM）がWebサイトのコンテンツをより良く理解するのに役立つ構造化ファイルです — robots.txtのAI版です。AIシステムによってサイトが発見・引用されやすくなります。

### LLMGeneratorはどのように動作しますか？

WebサイトのURLを入力するだけで、システムが自動的にサイトマップを使用してすべてのページを発見し、各ページからコンテンツを抽出し、適切にフォーマットされたllms.txtファイルを生成します。シンプル生成（1倍クレジット）またはAI最適化タイトル・説明付きの拡張生成（2倍クレジット）を選択できます。

### 料金はいくらですか？

透明なクレジットベースシステムを使用しています。シンプル生成では処理されたURL1つにつき1クレジット、拡張生成では2クレジットを支払います。小規模サイト（10 URL）は約$0.10-0.20、中規模サイト（25 URL）は$0.25-0.50です。クレジットは期限切れがなく、50の無料クレジットから始められます。

### 生成にはどのくらい時間がかかりますか？

ほとんどのWebサイトは、バックグラウンドジョブシステムを使用して2分以内に処理されます。リアルタイムの進捗更新を受け取り、完了次第すぐにファイルをダウンロードできます。大規模サイトは数分長くかかる場合があります。

### どのような種類のWebサイトがサポートされていますか？

ブログ、eコマースストア、ドキュメントサイト、企業サイトなど、一般にアクセス可能なほぼすべてのWebサイトをサポートしています。WordPress、Shopify、静的サイト、カスタムアプリケーションなどの人気プラットフォームで動作します。

### クレジットは期限切れになりますか？

いいえ、クレジットは期限切れになりません！これは私たちの主要な差別化要因の一つです。$1.99のクレジットパッケージを購入するか月額プランに加入するかに関わらず、未使用のクレジットは無期限にロールオーバーされます。実際に使用した分だけお支払いいただきます。

### APIアクセスは提供していますか？

はい！完全なREST APIアクセスはプロフェッショナルプラン（$14.99/月）以上の加入者に提供されています。APIを使用してllms.txt生成をCI/CDパイプラインに統合し、更新を自動化し、カスタムワークフローを構築できます。Python、JavaScript、cURLの完全なドキュメントとコード例が利用可能です。

### どのようなサポートを提供していますか？

すべてのユーザーにメールサポートと包括的なドキュメントへのアクセスを提供しています。有料加入者は応答時間の短い優先サポートを受けられます。BusinessとAgencyプランには専用サポートチャンネルが含まれています。通常24時間以内、多くの場合はるかに早く対応します。

AI検索が成長するにつれ、llms.txtはAI駆動の回答とレコメンデーションにコンテンツが表示されるのを助けます。

### 簡単な統合

プラグイン不要。生成されたファイルをWooCommerceサイトのルートディレクトリにアップロードするだけ。

## 対応するWooCommerce機能

- WordPress-based ecommerce
- Product variations
- Category hierarchies
- Extension ecosystem

## 解決する課題

- Complex product URLs with variations
- Category and tag archives
- Cart and checkout pages
- Dynamic pricing content

## WooCommerceユーザーへのメリット

1 Full product catalog with variations

2 Category structure preservation

3 WordPress content integration

4 Extension-added page detection

## 使い方

1

Enter your WooCommerce store URL

2

Products and variations are mapped

3

Categories create logical groupings

4

Download your ecommerce-optimized llms.txt

一般的なサイトサイズ: 50-1000 URL

## よくある質問

価格とプランについて知っておくべきすべて

### 料金はいくらですか？

透明なクレジットベースシステムを使用しています。シンプル生成では処理されたURL1つにつき1クレジット、拡張生成では2クレジットを支払います。小規模サイト（10 URL）は約$0.10-0.20、中規模サイト（25 URL）は$0.25-0.50です。クレジットは期限切れがなく、50の無料クレジットから始められます。

### クレジットは期限切れになりますか？

いいえ、クレジットは期限切れになりません！これは私たちの主要な差別化要因の一つです。$1.99のクレジットパッケージを購入するか月額プランに加入するかに関わらず、未使用のクレジットは無期限にロールオーバーされます。実際に使用した分だけお支払いいただきます。

### 生成にはどのくらい時間がかかりますか？

ほとんどのWebサイトは、バックグラウンドジョブシステムを使用して2分以内に処理されます。リアルタイムの進捗更新を受け取り、完了次第すぐにファイルをダウンロードできます。大規模サイトは数分長くかかる場合があります。

### APIアクセスは提供していますか？

はい！完全なREST APIアクセスはプロフェッショナルプラン（$14.99/月）以上の加入者に提供されています。APIを使用してllms.txt生成をCI/CDパイプラインに統合し、更新を自動化し、カスタムワークフローを構築できます。Python、JavaScript、cURLの完全なドキュメントとコード例が利用可能です。

### LLMGenerator Refund Policy

**URL:** https://llmgenerator.com/ja/refund/

# Refund Policy

Last updated: March 15, 2026

At LLMGenerator, we want you to feel confident every time you make
a purchase. This policy explains when and how refunds apply to our
credit packages and subscription plans.

## 1\. Credit Packages

Credits purchased as one-time packages are eligible for a full
refund within **7 days** of the
purchase date, provided that **no credits have been used**. Once any credits from a package have been consumed, that
package is no longer eligible for a refund.

Unused credits do not expire and carry over indefinitely, so we
encourage you to use them at your own pace.

## 2\. Subscription Plans

Monthly and annual subscription plans are eligible for a full
refund within **7 days** of the
initial charge or renewal date, as long as fewer than 20% of the plan's
monthly credits have been used during that billing period.

If you cancel a subscription after the 7-day window, your plan
will remain active until the end of the current billing period. No
partial refunds are issued for unused time within a billing
period.

## 3\. Used Credits

Credits that have been spent on generation jobs are **non-refundable**, regardless of the outcome. This includes:

- Successful llms.txt generations
- Partial generations (e.g., job interrupted due to site errors)

- Regenerations of previously generated files

If a generation fails due to an error on our side, we will restore
the credits used at no cost to you. Please contact us with the job
ID so we can investigate.

## 4\. Exceptions

We evaluate refund requests outside the standard policy on a
case-by-case basis for situations including:

- Duplicate charges caused by a billing system error
- Unauthorized charges reported within 30 days
- Technical failures on our infrastructure that prevented use of
the service


## 5\. How to Request a Refund

To request a refund, email us at [support@llmgenerator.com](mailto:support@llmgenerator.com) with the following information:

- The email address associated with your account
- The date of purchase and amount charged
- A brief description of the reason for your request

We aim to respond to all refund requests within **2 business days**. Approved refunds are processed back to the original payment
method and typically appear within 5–10 business days depending on
your bank or card issuer.

## 6\. Changes to This Policy

We reserve the right to update this Refund Policy at any time.
Material changes will be communicated via email or a notice on our
website. Continued use of the service after changes take effect
constitutes acceptance of the updated policy.

## 7\. Contact

Questions about this policy? Reach us at [support@llmgenerator.com](mailto:support@llmgenerator.com) or visit our [contact page](https://llmgenerator.com/contact).

### LLMGenerator Privacy Policy

**URL:** https://llmgenerator.com/ja/privacy/

# Privacy Policy

Last updated: March 15, 2026

## Introduction

LLMGenerator ("we", "our", or "us") is committed to protecting your
privacy. This Privacy Policy explains how we handle information when you
use our services, including the LLMGenerator website and browser
extensions.

## Browser Extension Privacy

The **LLMGenerator - LLM.txt Checker** browser extension is designed
with privacy as a core principle:

### What the Extension Does

- Checks if websites have `llms.txt` or `llms-full.txt` files at standard locations (`/`
  and `/docs/`)

- Displays a badge indicator showing the status of these files on the
   current website

- Provides quick access to view these files when they exist

### Data Collection

**We do not collect any personal data.** The extension:

- **Does NOT** collect, store, or transmit any personal information

- **Does NOT** track your browsing history

- **Does NOT** send any data to our servers

- **Does NOT** use cookies or any tracking technologies

- **Does NOT** have access to your passwords, form data, or any
   private information


### How the Extension Works

When you visit a website, the extension makes HTTP HEAD requests
directly to that website to check for the existence of `llms.txt`
files. These requests:

- Go directly from your browser to the website you're visiting
- Do not pass through any LLMGenerator servers
- Are identical to requests your browser makes for any normal file


### Local Caching

The extension temporarily caches check results in your browser's local
memory (not storage) for up to 10 minutes. This cache:

- Improves performance by reducing repeated requests
- Is stored only in your browser's RAM
- Is automatically cleared when you close your browser
- Is never transmitted anywhere

### Permissions Explained

The extension requests the following permissions:

- **activeTab**: Required to read the URL of the current
   tab to check for llms.txt files

- **host\_permissions (<all\_urls>)**: Required to make
   HEAD requests to any website you visit to check for llms.txt files


These permissions are used solely for the functionality described above
and nothing else.

## Website Privacy

When you visit the LLMGenerator website (llmgenerator.com), we may
collect:

### Analytics Data

We use analytics tools to understand how visitors use our website and
application, and to improve our services:

- **Google Analytics (via Google Tag Manager)**: Collects
   usage data such as pages visited, session duration, and traffic
   sources. Data is processed by Google under their Privacy Policy.

- **PostHog**: Product analytics to understand feature
   usage and user journeys across our website (llmgenerator.com) and app
   (app.llmgenerator.com). A shared cookie under `.llmgenerator.com` allows us to track the full user journey
   from marketing site to application.

- **Ahrefs Web Analytics**: Privacy-focused traffic
   analytics that does not use cookies or collect personal data.


### Information You Provide

If you join our waitlist or contact us, we collect:

- Email address (required)
- Name (optional)

This information is used only to:

- Send you updates about LLMGenerator when you've opted in
- Respond to your inquiries

## Third-Party Services

Our website and application use the following third-party services:

- **Cloudflare**: Website hosting, CDN, and DDoS protection

- **Cloudflare Turnstile**: Bot protection on forms (no
   personal data stored)

- **Google Analytics / Google Tag Manager**: Website and
   app usage analytics

- **PostHog**: Product analytics and user journey tracking
   across our website and app

- **Ahrefs Web Analytics**: Cookie-free traffic analytics

- **Stripe**: Payment processing (we do not store card
   data)

- **Resend**: Transactional email delivery


Each of these services has their own privacy policies that govern their
use of data.

## Data Security

We implement appropriate technical and organizational measures to
protect any information we process. However, no electronic transmission
or storage is 100% secure.

## Your Rights

You have the right to:

- Request access to any personal data we hold about you
- Request deletion of your personal data
- Unsubscribe from our mailing list at any time
- Uninstall the browser extension at any time

## Children's Privacy

Our services are not directed to children under 13. We do not knowingly
collect personal information from children under 13.

## Changes to This Policy

We may update this Privacy Policy from time to time. We will notify you
of any changes by posting the new Privacy Policy on this page and
updating the "Last updated" date.

## Contact Us

If you have any questions about this Privacy Policy, please contact us:

- Email: [support@llmgenerator.com](mailto:support@llmgenerator.com)
- Website: [https://llmgenerator.com](https://llmgenerator.com/)

* * *

### Terms of Service

**URL:** https://llmgenerator.com/ja/terms/

# Terms of Service

Last updated: March 15, 2026

## 1\. Acceptance of Terms

By accessing and using LLMGenerator ("the Service"), you
accept and agree to be bound by the terms and provision of
this agreement. If you do not agree to abide by the above,
please do not use this service.

## 2\. Description of Service

LLMGenerator is a web service that generates llms.txt files
for websites to improve their discoverability by Large
Language Models. The service includes:

- Website crawling and content extraction
- Automated llms.txt file generation
- File hosting and management
- API access for integration

## 3\. User Accounts

To use our service, you must:

- Provide accurate and complete registration information

- Maintain the security of your account credentials
- Be responsible for all activities under your account
- Notify us immediately of any unauthorized use

## 4\. Credit System and Billing

Our service operates on a credit-based system:

- Credits are consumed at a rate of 1 credit per URL processed

- Enhanced generation uses 2 credits per URL
- Credits never expire once purchased
- Refunds are available within 30 days of purchase
- Credits are refunded if generation fails due to our service


## 5\. Acceptable Use

You agree not to use the service to:

- Process websites you don't own or have permission to crawl

- Generate content that violates copyright or intellectual
property rights

- Attempt to reverse engineer or compromise our systems
- Use the service for illegal or harmful purposes
- Exceed reasonable usage limits or abuse the service

## 6\. Content and Intellectual Property

Regarding content and intellectual property:

- You retain ownership of your website content
- Generated llms.txt files belong to you
- You grant us permission to process your content to provide
the service

- We may use aggregated, anonymized data for service
improvement


## 7\. Service Availability

We strive to maintain high service availability but cannot
guarantee:

- 100% uptime or uninterrupted service
- Compatibility with all websites or content types
- Specific response times for processing
- Permanent storage of generated files

## 8\. Limitation of Liability

To the maximum extent permitted by law:

- We provide the service "as is" without warranties
- We are not liable for indirect, incidental, or consequential
damages

- Our total liability is limited to the amount you paid in the
last 12 months

- We are not responsible for third-party service failures


## 9\. Privacy and Data Protection

Your privacy is important to us. Please review our Privacy
Policy, which also governs your use of the service, to
understand our practices.

## 10\. Termination

Either party may terminate this agreement:

- You may delete your account at any time
- We may suspend or terminate accounts for violations
- Unused credits remain valid after account deletion
- Generated files may be retained according to our data
retention policy


## 11\. Changes to Terms

We reserve the right to modify these terms at any time. We
will notify users of material changes via email or service
notifications. Continued use of the service constitutes
acceptance of the modified terms.

## 12\. Governing Law

These terms are governed by the laws of Brazil. Any disputes
will be resolved through the competent courts of Brazil, in
accordance with Brazilian law.

## 13\. Contact Information

If you have any questions about these Terms of Service, please
contact us:

Email: [support@llmgenerator.com](mailto:support@llmgenerator.com)

✔ 無料で簡単
✔ 事実精度チェック

✔ 全LLMに対応

## なぜLLMテキストを検証するのか？

AI生成コンテンツには時々不正確さや不整合が含まれることがあります。当検証ツールは、公開やプロジェクトで使用する前にコンテンツの信頼性を保証します。

🤖

### AI信頼性

AI生成コンテンツが品質基準を満たし、誤解を招く情報を含まないことを保証します。

⚡

### コンテンツ品質

詳細なフィードバックと実用的な提案でAIコンテンツの全体的な品質を向上させます。

🚀

### 将来対応

現在および将来のAIモデルと基準に最適化されたコンテンツで先を行きます。

## Frequently Asked Questions

Everything you need to know about our pricing

### How does pricing work?

We use a transparent credit-based system with no hidden fees. Simple generation costs 1 credit per URL, Enhanced generation costs 2 credits per URL. A typical 25-page website costs $0.45-0.75 total. You can buy one-time credit packages or subscribe for monthly credits with rollover. All credits never expire.

### Do credits expire?

Never! All credits - whether from free tier, credit packages, or subscriptions - never expire. Subscription plans include monthly credit rollover (up to 2x your monthly allowance), so you're never rushed to use them. This is a key differentiator from competitors who often expire unused credits.

### How long does generation take?

Most websites complete in under 2 minutes. We use background processing with real-time progress updates so you can see exactly what's happening. Small sites (under 20 pages) often complete in 30 seconds. Larger sites may take a few minutes but you'll see live status updates throughout.

### Do you offer API access?

Yes! Full REST API access is available for Professional plan ($14.99/month) and above. Our API lets you integrate llms.txt generation into your CI/CD pipelines, automate updates, and build custom workflows. Complete documentation with code examples in Python, JavaScript, and cURL is available.

### LLMGenerator Privacy Policy

**URL:** https://llmgenerator.com/privacy/

# Privacy Policy

Last updated: March 15, 2026

## Introduction

LLMGenerator ("we", "our", or "us") is committed to protecting your
privacy. This Privacy Policy explains how we handle information when you
use our services, including the LLMGenerator website and browser
extensions.

## Browser Extension Privacy

The **LLMGenerator - LLM.txt Checker** browser extension is designed
with privacy as a core principle:

### What the Extension Does

- Checks if websites have `llms.txt` or `llms-full.txt` files at standard locations (`/`
  and `/docs/`)

- Displays a badge indicator showing the status of these files on the
   current website

- Provides quick access to view these files when they exist

### Data Collection

**We do not collect any personal data.** The extension:

- **Does NOT** collect, store, or transmit any personal information

- **Does NOT** track your browsing history

- **Does NOT** send any data to our servers

- **Does NOT** use cookies or any tracking technologies

- **Does NOT** have access to your passwords, form data, or any
   private information


### How the Extension Works

When you visit a website, the extension makes HTTP HEAD requests
directly to that website to check for the existence of `llms.txt`
files. These requests:

- Go directly from your browser to the website you're visiting
- Do not pass through any LLMGenerator servers
- Are identical to requests your browser makes for any normal file


### Local Caching

The extension temporarily caches check results in your browser's local
memory (not storage) for up to 10 minutes. This cache:

- Improves performance by reducing repeated requests
- Is stored only in your browser's RAM
- Is automatically cleared when you close your browser
- Is never transmitted anywhere

### Permissions Explained

The extension requests the following permissions:

- **activeTab**: Required to read the URL of the current
   tab to check for llms.txt files

- **host\_permissions (<all\_urls>)**: Required to make
   HEAD requests to any website you visit to check for llms.txt files


These permissions are used solely for the functionality described above
and nothing else.

## Website Privacy

When you visit the LLMGenerator website (llmgenerator.com), we may
collect:

### Analytics Data

We use analytics tools to understand how visitors use our website and
application, and to improve our services:

- **Google Analytics (via Google Tag Manager)**: Collects
   usage data such as pages visited, session duration, and traffic
   sources. Data is processed by Google under their Privacy Policy.

- **PostHog**: Product analytics to understand feature
   usage and user journeys across our website (llmgenerator.com) and app
   (app.llmgenerator.com). A shared cookie under `.llmgenerator.com` allows us to track the full user journey
   from marketing site to application.

- **Ahrefs Web Analytics**: Privacy-focused traffic
   analytics that does not use cookies or collect personal data.


### Information You Provide

If you join our waitlist or contact us, we collect:

- Email address (required)
- Name (optional)

This information is used only to:

- Send you updates about LLMGenerator when you've opted in
- Respond to your inquiries

## Third-Party Services

Our website and application use the following third-party services:

- **Cloudflare**: Website hosting, CDN, and DDoS protection

- **Cloudflare Turnstile**: Bot protection on forms (no
   personal data stored)

- **Google Analytics / Google Tag Manager**: Website and
   app usage analytics

- **PostHog**: Product analytics and user journey tracking
   across our website and app

- **Ahrefs Web Analytics**: Cookie-free traffic analytics

- **Stripe**: Payment processing (we do not store card
   data)

- **Resend**: Transactional email delivery


Each of these services has their own privacy policies that govern their
use of data.

## Data Security

We implement appropriate technical and organizational measures to
protect any information we process. However, no electronic transmission
or storage is 100% secure.

## Your Rights

You have the right to:

- Request access to any personal data we hold about you
- Request deletion of your personal data
- Unsubscribe from our mailing list at any time
- Uninstall the browser extension at any time

## Children's Privacy

Our services are not directed to children under 13. We do not knowingly
collect personal information from children under 13.

## Changes to This Policy

We may update this Privacy Policy from time to time. We will notify you
of any changes by posting the new Privacy Policy on this page and
updating the "Last updated" date.

## Contact Us

If you have any questions about this Privacy Policy, please contact us:

- Email: [support@llmgenerator.com](mailto:support@llmgenerator.com)
- Website: [https://llmgenerator.com](https://llmgenerator.com/)

* * *

GUSE LTDA - CNPJ: 47.758.713/0001-82

## Perguntas Frequentes

Tudo que você precisa saber sobre o LLMGenerator e llms.txt

### O que é llms.txt e por que preciso dele?

llms.txt é um arquivo estruturado que ajuda LLMs como ChatGPT, Claude, Gemini e Perplexity a entenderem melhor o conteúdo do seu site — como robots.txt mas para IA. Ele fornece um resumo estruturado do seu conteúdo que ajuda os LLMs a citar e recomendar seu site com precisão.

### Como funciona o LLMGenerator?

Digite a URL do seu site e descobrimos automaticamente todas as suas páginas usando seu sitemap (ou rastreamento inteligente como fallback). Extraímos o conteúdo de cada página e geramos um arquivo llms.txt adequadamente formatado. Escolha geração Simples (1x crédito) ou Aprimorada (2x créditos) com títulos e descrições otimizados por IA.

### Quanto custa?

Usamos um sistema transparente baseado em créditos. Você paga 1 crédito por URL processada para geração Simples, ou 2 créditos para geração Aprimorada. Sites pequenos (10 URLs) custam cerca de $0.10-0.20, sites médios (25 URLs) custam $0.25-0.50. Os créditos nunca expiram e você começa com 50 créditos grátis.

### Quanto tempo demora a geração?

A maioria dos sites é processada em menos de 2 minutos usando nosso sistema de jobs em background. Você receberá atualizações de progresso em tempo real e pode baixar seus arquivos imediatamente quando completo. Sites maiores podem demorar alguns minutos a mais.

### Que tipos de sites são suportados?

Suportamos praticamente qualquer site publicamente acessível incluindo blogs, lojas de e-commerce, sites de documentação, sites corporativos e mais. Nosso sistema funciona com plataformas populares como WordPress, Shopify, sites estáticos e aplicações customizadas.

### Os créditos expiram?

Não, os créditos nunca expiram! Este é um dos nossos principais diferenciais. Seja comprando um pacote de créditos de $1.99 ou assinando um plano mensal, seus créditos não utilizados são mantidos indefinidamente. Você paga apenas pelo que realmente usa.

### Vocês oferecem acesso à API?

Sim! Acesso completo à API REST está disponível para assinantes do plano Profissional ($14.99/mês) e superiores. Nossa API permite integrar a geração de llms.txt em pipelines CI/CD, automatizar atualizações e criar fluxos personalizados. Documentação completa com exemplos em Python, JavaScript e cURL está disponível.

### Que suporte vocês oferecem?

Todos os usuários têm suporte por email e acesso à nossa documentação abrangente. Assinantes pagos recebem suporte prioritário com tempos de resposta mais rápidos. Planos Business e Agency incluem canais de suporte dedicados. Normalmente respondemos em 24 horas, frequentemente muito mais rápido.

# About LLMGenerator

We help websites get discovered — not just by people, but by the AI
systems that increasingly shape what people find.

## Our Mission

The way content gets discovered is changing. AI assistants like
ChatGPT, Perplexity, and Claude are now the first stop for
millions of questions — and most websites aren't structured for
them to understand.

LLMGenerator exists to close that gap. We automatically crawl
your site and produce a clean, structured `llms.txt` file that tells AI systems exactly what your site is about, what
pages matter, and how to reference your content accurately.

No subscriptions you have to justify. No per-seat pricing. Just
a credit-based system where you pay for what you actually use.

50

Free credits to start

<2 min

Average generation time

3

Generation methods

99.9%

Uptime on Cloudflare

## What We Stand For

The principles behind every decision we make

### Transparent Pricing

Every credit has a clear cost. You always know exactly what
you're spending before you generate — no surprise invoices, no
usage tiers that reset at inconvenient times.

### Speed Without Compromise

Background processing lets us crawl more pages and produce
richer output than real-time tools. Most sites finish in under 2
minutes. Larger sites in under 5.

### Built to Last

Running on Cloudflare's global edge network with
enterprise-grade reliability. Your generated files are always
accessible, and your credits never expire.

## Why We Built This

We were early users of AI assistants for research, and we kept
running into the same frustration: great products and companies
would go completely unmentioned in AI answers, simply because
their sites weren't structured for LLMs to understand.

The `llms.txt` standard was already emerging, but implementing it properly required
either technical knowledge most site owners don't have, or expensive
agency work. There was no simple, affordable tool that could just
do it for you.

So we built one. LLMGenerator crawls your site, understands its
structure, and outputs a well-formed file that gives AI systems
the context they need to reference your content accurately — in
minutes, not days.

1

Crawl

We map your site's pages, content, and structure
automatically

2

Generate

Our engine produces a clean, AI-optimized llms.txt in the
background

3

Publish

Download the file and add it to your site's root — done

### Webflow llms.txt Guide

# Como Adicionar llms.txt ao Seu Site Webflow (Guia Passo a Passo)

Publicado:20 jan., 2026 \|  at 11:00 AM

# Como Adicionar llms.txt ao Seu Site Webflow (Guia Passo a Passo)

Webflow é o sonho de um designer - desenvolvimento visual poderoso sem comprometer código limpo. Se você está construindo no Webflow, adicionar um arquivo llms.txt requer uma abordagem personalizada, mas é totalmente viável e oferece controle total sobre sua otimização de IA.

Este guia abrangente irá orientá-lo através de dois métodos comprovados para implementar llms.txt no Webflow, desde a abordagem recomendada de página estática até soluções avançadas de código personalizado.

## Índice [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#%C3%ADndice)

## Por Que Sites Webflow Precisam de llms.txt [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#por-que-sites-webflow-precisam-de-llmstxt)

### A Vantagem do Designer [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#a-vantagem-do-designer)

Webflow oferece controle pixel-perfeito sobre o design do seu site. Um arquivo llms.txt estende esse controle para como sistemas de IA entendem e recomendam seu trabalho:

- **Visibilidade do Portfólio**: Ajude assistentes de IA a recomendar seu trabalho de design
- **Aquisição de Clientes**: Apareça quando clientes potenciais pedem designers à IA
- **Descoberta de Serviços**: Estrutura clara de suas ofertas
- **Showcase de Projetos**: Acesso organizado ao seu melhor trabalho

### Benefícios Específicos do Webflow [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#benef%C3%ADcios-espec%C3%ADficos-do-webflow)

- ✅ **Implementação Limpa**: Nenhum plugin inchado necessário
- ✅ **Controle Total**: Personalize exatamente como deseja
- ✅ **Carregamento Rápido**: Sobrecarga mínima
- ✅ **Profissional**: Estrutura semântica adequada

## Método 1: Abordagem de Página Estática (Recomendado) [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#m%C3%A9todo-1-abordagem-de-p%C3%A1gina-est%C3%A1tica-recomendado)

**Melhor para**: Maioria dos usuários Webflow
**Tempo necessário**: 10-15 minutos
**Nível técnico**: ⭐⭐ Moderado

Este método cria uma página dedicada que serve seu conteúdo llms.txt.

### Passo 1: Criar uma Nova Página Estática [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#passo-1-criar-uma-nova-p%C3%A1gina-est%C3%A1tica)

1. Abra seu projeto Webflow
2. No painel **Pages** (barra lateral esquerda), clique no **ícone +**
3. Clique em **Add Page**
4. Defina **Page Name**: `llms`
5. O sistema criará a URL `/llms`

### Passo 2: Configurar Definições da Página [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#passo-2-configurar-defini%C3%A7%C3%B5es-da-p%C3%A1gina)

1. Com a nova página selecionada, clique em **Settings** (ícone de engrenagem)
2. Na seção **SEO Settings**:

   - **Page Title**: Deixe em branco
   - **Meta Description**: Deixe em branco
   - **Slug**: Altere de `llms` para `llms.txt`
3. A URL agora é `/llms.txt`

### Passo 3: Projetar a Página [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#passo-3-projetar-a-p%C3%A1gina)

1. **Remover Elementos Padrão**
   - Exclua quaisquer seções ou contêineres padrão
   - Comece com uma página completamente em branco
2. **Adicionar um Elemento Code Embed**
   - Pressione `E` ou clique em **Add Elements** (+)
   - Role até **Components**
   - Arraste **Embed** para a página
   - Clique em **Edit Code** nas configurações de embed
3. **Adicionar Seu Conteúdo llms.txt**

   Cole este código:


```
<pre
  style="white-space: pre-wrap; font-family: monospace; margin: 0; padding: 0; width: 100%; background: white; color: black;"
>
# Nome do Seu Estúdio de Design

> Criando sites bonitos e funcionais para [seu nicho]

## Serviços

- [Web Design](/services/web-design) - Design de site personalizado
- [Branding](/services/branding) - Identidade de marca e sistemas de design
- [Desenvolvimento](/services/development) - Desenvolvimento Webflow

## Portfólio

- [Projetos em Destaque](/portfolio) - Nosso melhor trabalho
- [Galeria de Clientes](/clients) - Depoimentos e resultados de clientes
- [Estudos de Caso](/case-studies) - Análises detalhadas de projetos

## Sobre

- [Nosso Processo](/process) - Como trabalhamos
- [Sobre Nós](/about) - Nossa história e equipe
- [Contato](/contact) - Inicie seu projeto

## Recursos

- [Blog](/blog) - Insights de design e tutoriais
- [Recursos Gratuitos](/resources) - Templates e guias
- [FAQ](/faq) - Perguntas comuns respondidas
</pre>copiar
```

**Personalize** este template:

- Substitua o texto de espaço reservado pelo seu conteúdo real
- Atualize URLs para corresponder à estrutura do seu site
- Adicione/remova seções conforme necessário

### Passo 4: Definir Cabeçalho Content-Type [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#passo-4-definir-cabe%C3%A7alho-content-type)

Para garantir exibição de texto adequada, adicione isto ao código personalizado da sua página:

1. Com sua página llms selecionada, abra **Page Settings**
2. Vá para a aba **Custom Code**
3. Em **Before tag**, adicione:

```
<script>
  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    // Definir meta tag de tipo de conteúdo
    var meta = document.createElement("meta");
    meta.httpEquiv = "Content-Type";
    meta.content = "text/plain; charset=utf-8";
    document.head.appendChild(meta);
  });
</script>copiar
```

### Passo 5: Ocultar da Navegação [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#passo-5-ocultar-da-navega%C3%A7%C3%A3o)

1. Vá para o painel **Pages**
2. Clique com o botão direito em sua página `llms.txt`
3. Desmarque **Show in navigation**

Isso mantém acessível mas não visível nos menus.

### Passo 6: Publicar e Testar [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#passo-6-publicar-e-testar)

1. Clique em **Publish** (canto superior direito)
2. Selecione **Publish to \[seu domínio\]**
3. Abra uma nova aba e visite `seusite.com/llms.txt`
4. Verifique se o conteúdo é exibido como texto simples

## Método 2: Implementação de Código Personalizado (Avançado) [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#m%C3%A9todo-2-implementa%C3%A7%C3%A3o-de-c%C3%B3digo-personalizado-avan%C3%A7ado)

**Melhor para**: Desenvolvedores com acesso a hospedagem Webflow
**Tempo necessário**: 20-30 minutos
**Nível técnico**: ⭐⭐⭐ Avançado

Este método envolve exportar seu site e adicionar um arquivo `.txt` verdadeiro.

### Passo 1: Exportar Seu Site Webflow [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#passo-1-exportar-seu-site-webflow)

1. Vá para **Project Settings** \> **Export**
2. Clique em **Prepare ZIP**
3. Baixe os arquivos do seu site
4. Extraia o arquivo ZIP

### Passo 2: Criar Arquivo llms.txt [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#passo-2-criar-arquivo-llmstxt)

1. Abra um editor de texto (VS Code, Sublime, etc.)
2. Crie um novo arquivo chamado `llms.txt`
3. Adicione seu conteúdo em formato Markdown:

```
# Nome do Seu Site Webflow

> Descrição de uma frase do que você faz

## Serviços

- [Serviço 1](/service-1) - Descrição
- [Serviço 2](/service-2) - Descrição

## Trabalho

- [Portfólio](/portfolio) - Veja nossos projetos
- [Estudos de Caso](/case-studies) - Análises detalhadas

## Contato

- [Entre em Contato](/contact) - Inicie um projeto
- [Sobre](/about) - Conheça-noscopiar
```

### Passo 3: Adicionar ao Diretório Raiz [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#passo-3-adicionar-ao-diret%C3%B3rio-raiz)

1. Coloque `llms.txt` na raiz da pasta do seu site extraído
2. O arquivo deve estar no mesmo nível que `index.html`

### Passo 4: Re-enviar para Hospedagem [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#passo-4-re-enviar-para-hospedagem)

**Para Hospedagem Webflow**:
Infelizmente, a hospedagem Webflow não permite uploads diretos de arquivos. Você precisará:

1. Usar Método 1 (Página Estática) em vez disso, OU
2. Usar hospedagem personalizada (veja abaixo)

**Para Hospedagem Personalizada** (AWS, Netlify, Vercel, etc.):

1. Envie seus arquivos de site modificados
2. Configure seu host para servir `llms.txt` como `text/plain`
3. Implante

### Passo 5: Configurar Cabeçalhos do Servidor [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#passo-5-configurar-cabe%C3%A7alhos-do-servidor)

Adicione estes cabeçalhos para tipo de conteúdo adequado:

**Netlify** (arquivo `_headers`):

```
/llms.txt
  Content-Type: text/plain; charset=utf-8copiar
```

**Vercel** (`vercel.json`):

```
{
  "headers": [\
    {\
      "source": "/llms.txt",\
      "headers": [\
        {\
          "key": "Content-Type",\
          "value": "text/plain; charset=utf-8"\
        }\
      ]\
    }\
  ]
}copiar
```

**AWS S3**:
Defina metadados ao enviar: `Content-Type: text/plain`

## Templates Específicos do Webflow [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#templates-espec%C3%ADficos-do-webflow)

## Otimizando Seu llms.txt do Webflow [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#otimizando-seu-llmstxt-do-webflow)

### 1\. Mantenha a Estrutura Limpa [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#1-mantenha-a-estrutura-limpa)

A filosofia de código limpo do Webflow se estende ao seu llms.txt:

```
# Hierarquia clara

## Seções de segundo nível

### Terceiro nível se necessário

- Itens de lista com descriçõescopiar
```

### 2\. Combine com Seu Sistema de Design [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#2-combine-com-seu-sistema-de-design)

Organize seu llms.txt para espelhar a navegação do seu site:

- Principais serviços/produtos primeiro
- Portfólio/amostras de trabalho
- Sobre/informações da empresa
- Recursos e suporte

### 3\. Use Links Descritivos [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#3-use-links-descritivos)

```
❌ - [Clique aqui](/services)
✅ - [Serviços de Web Design](/services/web-design) - Sites personalizados para startupscopiar
```

### 4\. Aproveite o CMS do Webflow [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#4-aproveite-o-cms-do-webflow)

Se usar CMS do Webflow:

1. Crie um template CMS dedicado
2. Puxe conteúdo dinâmico
3. Atualize a página llms.txt automaticamente
4. Mantenha conteúdo fresco sem atualizações manuais

**Exemplo Dinâmico de CMS** (requer código personalizado):

```
<pre>
# {{ site-name }}

> {{ site-description }}

## Posts do Blog

{% for post in blog-posts %}
- [{{ post.title }}]({{ post.url }}) - {{ post.summary }}
{% endfor %}
</pre>copiar
```

### 5\. Teste em Todos os Breakpoints [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#5-teste-em-todos-os-breakpoints)

Como você está usando o design responsivo do Webflow:

1. Teste llms.txt em desktop, tablet, mobile
2. Certifique-se de que a tag `<pre>` é exibida corretamente
3. Verifique o tratamento de espaços em branco
4. Verifique se não há rolagem horizontal

## Melhores Práticas Específicas do Webflow [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#melhores-pr%C3%A1ticas-espec%C3%ADficas-do-webflow)

### 1\. Use as Configurações de SEO do Webflow [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#1-use-as-configura%C3%A7%C3%B5es-de-seo-do-webflow)

Otimize páginas que aparecem no seu llms.txt:

1. Vá para **Page Settings** para cada página
2. Adicione **Page Title** claro
3. Escreva **Meta Description** concisa
4. Use tags **OpenGraph** adequadas

### 2\. Organize Coleções [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#2-organize-cole%C3%A7%C3%B5es)

Se usar Coleções CMS do Webflow:

1. Crie estruturas de coleção lógicas
2. Use nomes de coleção claros
3. Adicione descrições a itens de coleção
4. Link para páginas principais de coleção no llms.txt

### 3\. Aproveite Interações (Com Cuidado) [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#3-aproveite-intera%C3%A7%C3%B5es-com-cuidado)

Mantenha sua página llms.txt simples:

- ❌ Sem animações ou interações
- ❌ Sem layouts complexos
- ✅ Texto simples e legível
- ✅ Carregamento rápido

### 4\. Controle de Versão [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#4-controle-de-vers%C3%A3o)

Como Webflow não tem controle de versão integrado:

1. Mantenha uma cópia do seu conteúdo llms.txt externamente
2. Documente mudanças em uma planilha
3. Anote o que você atualiza e quando
4. Rastreie mudanças de desempenho

### 5\. Sites de Clientes [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#5-sites-de-clientes)

Se construindo para clientes:

1. Explique benefícios do llms.txt em sua proposta
2. Inclua configuração em seus entregáveis
3. Mostre aos clientes como atualizar conteúdo
4. Adicione ao seu pacote de manutenção do site

## Problemas Comuns do Webflow e Soluções [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#problemas-comuns-do-webflow-e-solu%C3%A7%C3%B5es)

### Problema: Página Mostra Branding Webflow [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#problema-p%C3%A1gina-mostra-branding-webflow)

**Problema**: Rodapé padrão Webflow aparece

**Solução**:

1. Remova todas as seções da página
2. Use apenas o elemento embed
3. Defina preenchimento da página como 0
4. Oculte navegação/rodapé do site para esta página

### Problema: Conteúdo Não é Texto Simples [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#problema-conte%C3%BAdo-n%C3%A3o-%C3%A9-texto-simples)

**Problema**: Texto estilizado ou formatação Webflow visível

**Solução**:

1. Use tag `<pre>` com estilos inline
2. Defina `background: white` e `color: black`
3. Remova todas as classes Webflow
4. Use o componente embed, não blocos de texto

### Problema: Não Consegue Alterar Extensão da URL [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#problema-n%C3%A3o-consegue-alterar-extens%C3%A3o-da-url)

**Problema**: Webflow adiciona barra final

**Solução**:

1. Defina slug da página exatamente como `llms.txt`
2. Webflow irá servir em `/llms.txt`
3. Teste com e sem barra final
4. Ambos devem funcionar

### Problema: 404 no Domínio Personalizado [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#problema-404-no-dom%C3%ADnio-personalizado)

**Problema**: Funciona em webflow.io mas não no domínio personalizado

**Solução**:

1. Republique o site completamente
2. Limpe o cache do Webflow
3. Verifique configurações DNS
4. Aguarde 10-15 minutos para propagação

### Problema: Problemas de Exibição Mobile [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#problema-problemas-de-exibi%C3%A7%C3%A3o-mobile)

**Problema**: Texto muito pequeno ou formatado estranhamente no mobile

**Solução**:

```
<pre
  style="
  white-space: pre-wrap;
  font-family: monospace;
  font-size: 14px;
  line-height: 1.6;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 20px;
  width: 100%;
  overflow-x: auto;
"
>
Seu conteúdo aqui
</pre>copiar
```

## Otimização de Desempenho [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#otimiza%C3%A7%C3%A3o-de-desempenho)

### Mantenha Leve [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#mantenha-leve)

Seu llms.txt deve carregar instantaneamente:

- Tamanho de arquivo alvo: < 10KB
- Sem imagens ou recursos externos
- Estrutura HTML mínima
- Entrega de conteúdo direto

### Estratégia de Cache [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#estrat%C3%A9gia-de-cache)

Configure cabeçalhos de cache (se em hospedagem personalizada):

```
Cache-Control: public, max-age=3600copiar
```

Isso armazena em cache por 1 hora - equilíbrio entre frescor e desempenho.

### Considerações sobre CDN [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#considera%C3%A7%C3%B5es-sobre-cdn)

Se usar Cloudflare ou outra CDN:

1. Certifique-se de que llms.txt passa pela CDN
2. Defina regras de cache apropriadas
3. Configure como tipo de conteúdo `text/plain`
4. Limpe o cache após atualizações

## Testando Sua Implementação Webflow [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#testando-sua-implementa%C3%A7%C3%A3o-webflow)

### Lista de Verificação de Teste Abrangente [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#lista-de-verifica%C3%A7%C3%A3o-de-teste-abrangente)

1. **Acesso Direto**
   - ✅ `seusite.com/llms.txt` carrega
   - ✅ Conteúdo exibido como texto simples
   - ✅ Sem elementos UI do Webflow visíveis
2. **Validação de Conteúdo**
   - ✅ Todos os links são absolutos ou relativos
   - ✅ Links vão para páginas corretas
   - ✅ Sem links quebrados
   - ✅ Formatação Markdown correta
3. **Teste Cross-Browser**
   - ✅ Chrome
   - ✅ Firefox
   - ✅ Safari
   - ✅ Edge
4. **Teste de Dispositivos**
   - ✅ Desktop
   - ✅ Tablet
   - ✅ Mobile
5. **Desempenho**
   - ✅ Carrega em < 1 segundo
   - ✅ Sem erros no console
   - ✅ Cabeçalho content-type adequado

## Manutenção e Atualizações [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#manuten%C3%A7%C3%A3o-e-atualiza%C3%A7%C3%B5es)

### Cronograma de Atualização Regular [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#cronograma-de-atualiza%C3%A7%C3%A3o-regular)

**Mensalmente**:

- Revise para novas páginas/projetos
- Atualize descrições
- Verifique links quebrados

**Trimestralmente**:

- Auditoria completa de conteúdo
- Reestruture se necessário
- Teste todas as funcionalidades

**Após Atualizações Importantes**:

- Lançamento de novo produto/serviço
- Redesign do site
- Novas peças de portfólio

### Processo de Atualização [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#processo-de-atualiza%C3%A7%C3%A3o)

1. **Editar no Webflow Designer**
   - Abra sua página llms
   - Edite o código embed
   - Salve alterações
2. **Teste na Visualização**
   - Use modo de visualização do Webflow
   - Verifique formatação
   - Verifique links
3. **Publicar**
   - Publique em seu site
   - Limpe cache
   - Teste versão ao vivo

### Rastreamento de Versão [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#rastreamento-de-vers%C3%A3o)

Mantenha um registro de alterações:

```
## Registro de Alterações llms.txt

### 2025-10-05

- Adicionado novo serviço: Desenvolvimento E-commerce
- Atualizada seção de portfólio
- Corrigido link quebrado para página sobre

### 2025-09-15

- Implementação inicial
- Adicionados todos os serviços principais
- Criada seção de portfóliocopiar
```

## Conclusão: Webflow + llms.txt = Escolha do Designer [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#conclus%C3%A3o-webflow--llmstxt--escolha-do-designer)

A flexibilidade do Webflow torna a implementação do llms.txt simples, ao mesmo tempo que oferece controle completo de design. Seja escolhendo o método de página estática ou abordagem de hospedagem personalizada, você está posicionando seu site para descoberta por IA.

### Plano de Ação Rápido [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#plano-de-a%C3%A7%C3%A3o-r%C3%A1pido)

**Hoje (15 minutos)**:

1. ✅ Crie página llms.txt no Webflow
2. ✅ Adicione seu conteúdo usando o método embed
3. ✅ Configure as definições da página
4. ✅ Publique e teste

**Esta Semana (30 minutos)**:

1. ✅ Otimize descrições de páginas
2. ✅ Teste todos os links
3. ✅ Revise exibição mobile
4. ✅ Adicione ao robots.txt

**Contínuo**:

1. ✅ Atualize mensalmente com novo conteúdo
2. ✅ Monitore análises
3. ✅ Refine com base em resultados

### Principais Conclusões [\#](https://llmgenerator.com/pt-br/blog/como-adicionar-llms-txt-webflow/\#principais-conclus%C3%B5es)

- ✅ Webflow torna llms.txt possível sem plugins
- ✅ Método de página estática funciona para maioria dos usuários
- ✅ Mantenha estrutura limpa e simples
- ✅ Teste minuciosamente em dispositivos
- ✅ Atualize regularmente com novo conteúdo

Seu site Webflow merece ser descoberto por assistentes de IA. Com estes métodos, você pode implementar llms.txt hoje e apresentar seu trabalho para a próxima geração de pesquisadores.
